After the conclusion of their work, he showered them with gifts and travel provisions, had them return to their compatriots, and sent with them his messengers with gifts for their governors and clothing for their leader * Eleazar, the high priest, and their companions; in his letter he praised their work. The scholarly translators asked him for one of these copies to be able to boast of it before their compatriots; and Ptolemy granted it to them. This was the decree of the Providence of God who, in His eternal


[p. 645] {#po.5.p645}
اصحاهم ففعل ذلك بم هذا كان تدين الله لما نقتم وسبق. فى علمه مما سيكون من فعل كبنتهم ومدبريهم حنان وقيافا واصحاهما من البغى على المسيح وقت ظبوره وزمان مجيه ' وتعرضيم لقتله! على ما هومكتوب فى الانجيل عند ذوى النصارى ثم ظبرت قيامة المسيح ولقيه بش كثين منهم يعتدون بمننه عليهم واحسانه اليهم من اقامته الموتى وابرابه البرص3 وفله العجايب التى كانت تببن العقول وتنتّ الذهول وتميت المعقول وتحيى المجبول تحقيقا لما قروا عليه من كتب الانياة متكت الود بما غيروا من الكتب فحني احلا على الكبنة فى امن المسبح واخافوهم على انفهم هوا بقتلي واقاموا علهم عند ذلك تحايدوا عنهم وتنحوا من بين ايديهم واعملوا اراهم فى وضع الحجج ليم فى امن المسيج ليدفعوا بها عن انفسهم9 وكانت خزاين الكتب فى اينهم
wisdom (knew) the future act of Annas and Caiaphas, their high priests and leaders, and of their partisans, who acted in an iniquitous manner against the Messiah at the time of his appearance, of his advent * and of their participation in his death, according to what is written in the Gospel of the Christians. Then arrived the resurrection of Christ. Several of them joined him, counting on his goodness and his mercy toward them, because he raised the dead, healed the lepers, and performed miracles, which struck the mind, troubled the reason, confounded the intelligence, made the inexplicable live, in support of what was written on this subject in the Books of the Prophets. The Jews divulged what they had changed in the sacred Books. Then they revolted against the high priests * in the question of Christ, threatened their lives, proposed to kill them, and attacked them. In the meantime, the high priests changed tactics to escape them and turned their attention to inventing for themselves proofs in the question of the Messiah * to protect their lives by this means. The libraries being in their hands, they occupied themselves, as


[p. 646] {#po.5.p646}
فشمدوا كالذى ذكرنا! انها إلى التوراة ونظروا فيعا بعت الفابة فيباا من زمانهم وما قاب من ادم وزمانه فاسقطوا من سنى ادم ومن بعذه الى منتبى ميلاد ابراهيم الفا وثلئماية وضع وثمانين سنة من كل واحد ممن اسقطوا من سنيه ” المعدودة والمحسوبة من دهرهم المتقادمة قبل ان يولد لهم الاولاد ماية سنة ماية سنة وتقلوها الى مدة سنى عيشهم من بعد ان ولد لبم الاولاد التى لاتحسب ولاتعدة فى نسبة الدهور ومدة الزمان ولوجودهم قبنان ابن ارفحشد ابن سام ابن نوح اسقطوا اسمه من كتاب التوراة واسقطوا سنيه واسقاط هذد السنين من مواضعها يّن ظاهر لمن بيصر اذا هو عاين المواضع وفحص عنه من كتاب التوراة وذلك الموضع يشهد لنفسه يما فد فيه وتقص منه فلما فعلوا ذلك واحكموه خفية دعوا7 بعض من يقم عليهم فى افعالهم بالمسيخ واحتجوا عندهم بان زمانه لم .يات بعد ولا .بلغ ظبوره دون اخرالدهر قالوا نحن" بعد فى
we have already said above, with the Torah with a preconceived intention and observed there that the end of their era was very far from the era of Adam, so they subtracted from the years of Adam and his descendants, until the birth of Abraham, 1389 years, after having subtracted from the years * in a determined number of the life of each (of the patriarchs) the years prior to the birth of their children, one hundred years from each, and transferred them to the years of their life after * the birth of their children, * which do not count at the beginning of time. Having found Cainan, son of Arphaxad, son of Shem, son of Noah, they subtracted his name from the book of the Torah and his years. The subtraction of these years from their place is evident to anyone who will see with his own eyes these passages and will examine this question according to the Torah; this passage will indicate to him what they have mutilated and subtracted from it. When they had done it and arranged it in secret, they invited a certain number of those who had revolted against them, because of their way of acting in the question of the Messiah, and alleged to them proofs claiming that his time had not yet arrived and that his appearance would only take place in the last days of the world; * they said: "We are still in the middle of the space of the years of the


[p. 647] {#po.5.p647}
وسط المدة من ستى العالم ثم قالوا هذه التورية يننا وينكم فاحضروا كتاب التورية وقد افدت باسقاطهم تلك السنين فيها وشككوهم بذلك ودفعوهم به عن انفسهم ووضعوا التوراة فى ذلك نسخا شتى! ودسوها الى ثقاتهم فى اطرافهم ونواحى ما حوليم ليظهروا اقراتها وتقيموا حجتهم فيها من الفساد والنقصان وهى اليوم فى ابدى عامة النصارى مقن يستعمل قراة السزيانية ولم يكن ظهرت ليم التوراة المصححة التى فسرها البعون الى ان ملك ؛ قسطنطين ابن هلانى المومن وكان ملكه بعد مجى اسبح ثائماية سنة وس سنين فقدم الى يت المقدس وطلب اثار المسيح وكتب الانيا لاتخاذها والاتفاع منها4 فدفع اليه الهود جميع الكتب وكان كتاب التوربة الفاسدة فيما دفعوا اليه وقبل ذلك ما كان وقع الخلاف ينهم من بعض من كان يخاف منهم ان يظهر الاقرار بالسيح ونا جا به فاندس اوليك الى قسطنطين الملك واعلموه فساد التوراة التى دفعوا اليه وفشهم له فى دقلك7
world." Then they added: "Let this Torah be between us and you!" and they had the book of the Torah brought, which had already been mutilated by their reduction of these years; with this they cast them into doubt * and turned them away from what they were. After that they composed separate copies of this Torah, which they handed over in secret to trusted persons in their countries and in the neighboring provinces, so that they might recite it and support their arguments in favor of the corruption and the reduction. Even today this Torah is in the hands of all the Christians who use the Syriac language. The true Torah, translated by the Seventy, was not shown to them until the reign * of Constantine, son of Helena, the faithful, who reigned 305 years after the advent of Christ. * Having gone to Jerusalem, he asked for the relics of Christ and the books of the Prophets to choose from them and profit from them. The Jews gave him all the books; and among the books that were given to him was found the book of the mutilated Torah. Before that, no discord had broken out among them; but there were some of them who were afraid that the truth about the Messiah and about what had happened to him would be discovered. Then these intrigued with King Constantine and informed him of the corruption of the Torah that the Jews had given him, and their perfidy


[p. 648] {#po.5.p648}
وان النسخة التى قلم! با السبعين المفسرين قبليم مخفية وان ليا نظير فى مدينة الاسكندرية ورومية وما بينهما من المداين فعث الى كبنة اليبود فاعلمهم ما وقع اليه فاتكروا ذلك وجحدوا معرفته قامن بهم الى الحبس وحث زسله الى الامكندربة ورومية وغيرهما من ياتيه4 بالنسخة فبلغ ذلك الكبنا المحبوسين وتخوفوا على انفسهم فدسوا تلك النسخة الى بعض مشيختم الدهريين وسالوهم اطلاع قشطنطين الملك على أمرها؟ بعد ان ياخذوا ليم منه الامان ففعلوا” ذلك وحملوا النسخة اليه من بعد ايام قامس باخراج الكبنة من الحبس توافت اليه نسختها من الاسكندرية ورومية9 وغيرهما فجمع ينهما فاصابهما قياسا وكلام واحد ثم دعا بالتوراة الفاسدةة فوجد فادها ظاهرا ينا رجلا رجلا وسنبهم الماية الماية التى تقلت من اوايل سنيهم المحسوة لمدة تاريخ سنى العالم الى اخر
toward him on this report; they informed him that the copy, made before them by the Seventy commentators, * had been hidden, that there were still (copies) similar to this one in Alexandria, in Rome, and in the cities that were between them. The emperor sent to the high priests of the Jews, informing them of what had happened to him. They disapproved of it and denied his information. Then Constantine, having ordered them to be thrown into prison, sent his messengers to Alexandria, to Rome, and to other cities so that the copy might be brought to him. This reached the imprisoned high priests, who were afraid for their lives. Then they handed over in secret this copy to some of their impious leaders and asked them to inform Emperor Constantine of this affair, after having obtained from him a pardon for them. They did so and, a few days later, they handed him the copy. The emperor ordered the release * of the high priests. The copies from Alexandria, from Rome, and from other cities having reached him, he compared them and found them to be of one manner and one language. Then having asked for the mutilated Torah, he found in it the clear and evident alteration, man after man, and their years


[p. 649] {#po.5.p649}
سنهم التى لا تحب قبل ان يولد ليم الاولاد1 وكاات العلة التتى دعت قسطنطين الى طلب كتب التورية والانيا مئلته اياهم قبل ذلك عما وجد فى كتاب دانيال النبى من وصفه3 ونته وقت ظبور المسبح وقتله4 بعدة منتهى سبع سوايع والنين وستين سابوعا من بعد السبعين سنة التى مكنثها بنو اسراييل بارض بابل كلام جبرابيل الملاك لدانيال النبى بذلك ونعته9 له وتقدمه اليه فى التفهم لقوله من وقت سماعة كلام له واستئنا عليه فى القول بظبور المسيح وقتله7 عند تمام تلك السوايع من الحدود التى وصف جبرايل الملاك لدانال النبى من كلامه له وانصراف بنى اسرابيل من بابل ونيان بيت المقدس؟ فلما عرف قسطططين الماك تلفيق النهود وحاله منطقهم فى استجاجيم بان قدما ملوكهم كانوا يسمون المسوحين9! فقال ليم ومن منهم كان يسمى المسيح ظهر بعد !! انصراف بنى اسراييل من بابل لتمام هذه *! السوابيع فوقفوا فى احابته باهتين متحيرين
hundred after hundred, which had been transferred from their first years which count in the chronology of the world, before the birth of their children, to the last years which do not count. That is the cause that made Emperor Constantine ask for the books of the Torah and * of the Prophets: previously he had asked them what was found in the Book of the prophet Daniel * concerning the information and indications on the time of the advent of the Messiah and of his death at the end of the seven weeks and the sixty-two weeks, after the 70 years during which the Israelites had remained in the land of Babel, and on the address * of the archangel Gabriel to the prophet Daniel on this report, on his mission, on the order he gave him to understand his word well, while he would pronounce his address, on his exposition of the advent of the Messiah and of his death at the end of these weeks, within the limits that the archangel Gabriel had fixed for the prophet Daniel in the discourse he addressed to him, and on the departure of the Israelites from Babel and the construction of Jerusalem. * When Emperor Constantine had learned of the lie of the Jews and the cunning of their language in their arguments drawn from the fact that their ancient kings had called themselves anointed, he said to them: "Who among them called himself the Messiah (the anointed) and appeared after the departure of the Israelites from


[p. 650] {#po.5.p650}
ثم قالوا ان المسيح المتنبى عليه ! يانى فى اخر الازمان والدهور ونحن تتظرة بعد لاناة فى وسط المدة فقال ليم وفى+ كم انتم من المدة يومكم هذا فقالوا له5 منتى المدة سبع الف سنة ونحن9 بعد7 فى نحو من اربعة الف سنة فرد عليم بالتكذيب ليم حيث راى8 حراتهم على الكذب9 فند ذلك بعث الى الاساقفة فاخرهم بالقصة وباعتنايه بما وجد فى نون دانال النبى على المسيح وما راى من ضعف حجة البود فى ذلك وسال الاساقفة ان يوضحوا له10 الحجة فيه وان الاساقفة استنظرى الكلام فى ذلك الى !! وقت خلوته! ولاعجابه الكلام فيه والتشوق الى علمه ومعرفته اخلى ليم مجلسه استلذاذا لمحاجتهم البهود فيه وامرهم جميع بالكلام واخبر الاساقفة بقول اليبود " واقروا له بذلك فقال الاساقفة ان13 تحايد الهود عن قول الحق فى امن المنيح ايا الملك متقدم! بورانة من اوليهم ة! وجحدهم امرا تخوفا " لقص دينهم والزامهم ما يلزم اهل
Babel at the end of these weeks?" But they did not answer because they were all disconcerted and embarrassed. Then they said that the Messiah announced by the prophets would come in the last times: "We are waiting for him later, because we are in the middle of this time." He said to them: "How much time do you count in this day?" * They said to him: "The term of time is 7000 * years, and we are still in the year 4000 approximately." Then the emperor answered them and accused them of deceit, when he saw their boldness in lying. In the meantime he sent messengers to the bishops, to inform them of this story and of the interest he had found in the prophecy of the prophet Daniel concerning the Messiah, and to share with them what he had perceived of the weakness of the arguments of the Jews on this report. He asked the bishops to clearly expose to him arguments on this subject. * The bishops asked him for a delay to treat this subject until the time of the private audience. He was astonished by this language and in his desire to know and understand it, he assigned them an audience; for he took pleasure in their dispute with the Jews on this subject and ordered them all to speak. He informed the bishops of the word of the Jews * who confirmed it. Then the bishops said: "The deviation of the Jews from the word of truth, O king, in the question of the Messiah has lasted since ancient times,


[p. 651] {#po.5.p651}
الفدران! والظلم والحجة فيا اتعوا من مدة النتين من ادم فليم ظاهرة “بين الفا نظرت فيا وكشفت عن الحقايق منها عرفته فقد رفنا النسختين اليك ويا لك فيهالا جميع وشرحنا تفسير السبعين الحكيم الذين فسروا التوراة لبطلميوس قلياذلفس الملك قبل مجى المسيح سيدنا ووقت ظبوره نحو من ثاثماية سنة ونسخة التوراة التى فى ايدى الهود اليوم " على ما+ افسدوا اولهم حنان وقيافا رئسا كهنتهم فى زمان المسيح ونقصوا منها من السنين مانقصوا ومن سوى ذلك فان انا حجة ظاهرة ناتى علها برهان ويان وفيها طول كلام فان ياذن لنا الملك قصصنا الحديث فيا وشرحنا بلغ السنين من سوابيع دانيال النبى الى منتهى قتل7 المسيح زمانا زمانا وملكا ملكا فقال لهم الملك افعلوا ذلك فقال الاساقفة ان9 نى الله0! تتبى على خراب بيت المقدس هدم حايطها وسبى اهلبا الى بابل ومقامهم ها سبعين عام فلما ملك يخنصرا! على بابل غزا بيت
as an inheritance from their first (high priests). Their refusal to believe in his mission for fear * of altering their religion pushed them to acts worthy of deceitful and impious men. The argument that they allege from the number of years since Adam is only just in appearance. You have examined it and have elucidated the bottom of it which you have well understood. We have given you two manuscripts where we have explained everything to you; we have related the translation of the Seventy scholars who had translated the Torah under King Ptolemy Philadelphus, 300 years approximately before the advent of the Messiah Our Lord and the time of his appearance. The copy of the Torah which is found today between the hands of the Jews * is made after the one that Annas and Caiaphas, their high priests, mutilated at the time of the Messiah and from which they subtracted the years that they reduced. Besides that, we have clear arguments that we will bring with proofs and explanation and whose exposition will be long; but if the emperor permits it, we will make the report of it * and will explain the term of the years according to the weeks of the prophet Daniel until the death of the Messiah, era by era, king by king." Then the emperor said to them: "Do it." The bishops said: "(Daniel), the prophet of God, prophesied the destruction of Jerusalem, the demolition of its walls, the captivity of its people in Babylon, their stay there for 70 years. When King Bokht-Nassar (Nebuchadnezzar) reigned over Babylon, he attacked Jerusalem, and took into


[p. 652] {#po.5.p652}
المقدن ونا طابفة من اعلا وكان فم دائيال الى فلما نزل يختتصر خروها فى فرط السنين منتهى عشرين عاما من بعد غزوته الاولى ! وفى اخر غزواته هدم حابطبا واحرق هيكلبا بلنار واستوعب سبى اهلها واخريها وان دانيال النبى بعد خمنين عام ذكر قول الله فيما نتى3 به ارميا النبى فى وقت مقامهم بابل فتيّن له ان قد قرب الوقت فد ذلك ما اعمل نفسه8 فى الصلاة لريه وادامتها بالتضرع اليه بالصيام واللكآ والخشوع ودعا حزن وهو مكتوب فى كتاب .دانيال النبى حيث كان بالاقرار منه لريه باساةًة اصحابه وتعديد ذنوهم وسله لهم العفو والمغفرة9 فصام واحد وعشرين يوم لم ياكل فيها7 خزا ولم شرب فيبا ماء ولم يضطجع وذكر فى صلاته عدة الله لبنى اسرايل فى وحيه لانيائه بميجى المسيح لقوام نياتهم وصلاحيم وسله ان يظهر له رجاه من ذلك. ومتى تكون رحعتم الى بيت المقدس فاستجاب الله دعا لحسن ايمانه وذكا9 قلبه وصدق نيته فيما اخلص0! له من الدعا فاظهر الله له مسلته لان الحق بالحقايق تجرى وتحقيقه بضى ولاستيثاق !1
captivity the greatest part of the population, * among which was found the prophet Daniel. Not ceasing to attack it during the 20 years that followed his first invasion, Nebuchadnezzar demolished, in his last invasion, its walls, burned its temple, seized its entire people, and devastated the country. And here it is that after 50 years the prophet Daniel remembered the word of God that the prophet Jeremiah * had announced during their stay in Babel. It was evident to him that this time was already near. In the meantime he absorbed himself in prayer to God in fasting, tears, with humility and compunction. It is written in * the Book of Daniel the prophet: He confessed before his Lord the wicked actions of his companions, enumerated their sins, and implored his pardon and his indulgence for them. He fasted for twenty-one days, without eating bread, without drinking water, and without lying down. In his prayer he recalled the promise of God to the Israelites to reveal to his prophets the advent of the Messiah to support their good will and lead them to good; he prayed to God to (show) him the realization of this hope and to indicate to him the time of their return to Jerusalem. God answered his prayer because of his good faith, the uprightness of his heart, and the purity of his intentions in the request he addressed to Him. * God revealed to him what


[p. 653] {#po.5.p653}
داتال الب فى طلنة وحق! كشف القطا عما غاب عنه وجهلة فعث الله اليه جبرايل ريس ملايكته لبينه عن مساته فقال .له جبرابيل الملاك3 ليتتن الروبا وقول الانبيا وللمسيج قدس القديسين فاعلم فيم يادانيالك من مخرج الكلمة وبين قولى ,ارجل الشبوة ولترجعن ولننين بيت المقدى والملك المسيح الى وات مجيه وقنله سيع سوايع واتين وستين سابوعا ثم يقتل ومدينة القدس تخرب فيدا حبراييل الملاك اول فقال لدانيال الروبا وقول الانيا انه يتم معنى ذلك رجعة بنى اسراييل من بعد سبعين عاما تحقيقا لقول ارميا النبى ومن بعد رجعتبم تبنا يت المقدس ومن بعد بايها تحسب سوايع المسيح منتبى قتله4 وكقول حبرايل الملاك فى ذلك كذلك فمل الله بهم بعدل_منه وصدق لقوله وكما طال انحدار بنى اسراييل الى بابل عشرين عاما دفعة بعد دفعة كذلك ورجعتهم طالت وانتبت من بعد عشرين عاما دفعة من بعد دفعة واستوت سنو الجميع فى مقامهم يابل سبعين عاما فكمل؟ الاستوا كقول النبى وكانت الدفعة الاولى توافت الى
he was asking for, * because the true follows sincerity * and certainty comes to light: and because of the faithfulness of Daniel, He answered his request and lifted the veil of what had been hidden from his eyes and which he had not known. God sent him the archangel Gabriel to explain his request to him. The angel Gabriel said to him: "The vision and the word of the prophets concerning the Messiah, the Holy of Holies, are going to be accomplished. Learn and grasp, O Daniel, the word that is uttered, and may my word be unveiled to you, O man of desire: you will return and rebuild Jerusalem. As for the reign of the Messiah, until the time of his advent and his death will elapse seven weeks * and sixty-two weeks. Then he will be put to death and the holy city will be destroyed." Thus began the angel Gabriel; then he said to Daniel: "The vision and the word of the prophets will be accomplished, that is to say in what concerns the return of the Israelites at the end of 70 years, in accordance with the word of the prophet Jeremiah. After their return, Jerusalem will be rebuilt and after its restoration the weeks of the Messiah will be counted until his death." God treated the Israelites as * Gabriel had said to attest to the sincerity and the truth of his word. The transplantation of the Israelites to Babel lasted for 20 years and took place on different occasions; their return lasted and was also completed in 20 years and took place on different occasions; * the total duration of their stay in Babel being 70 years,


[p. 654] {#po.5.p654}
بابل فى السنة الثانية من مملكة! بخص واقاموا فيها قية ملكة وزمان" حيانة ثلثة وارعين سنة ومن بعد فى مملكة اهل بيته خمس اعوام ومن بعدهم فى مملكة كوس الفااسى اثنين وعشرون عاما فذلك سبعين عامًا كقول الى ولدفمة الاذرى من السبى4 توافت بابل لاننين وعشرين عام مضت من ملك بختنصر فقاموا بابل الثاثة والمشرين العام الباقية من ملكه ثم مملكة اهل بيته خمسة اعوام وعد ذلك مملكة كورس الفارسى واحد وثلثين عاما ولقمديسوس الملك ثمنية اعوام وللمجوس عاما واحداً ولداربوس ابن يوشتاصف 9" عامين فذلك سعين :عام فتمت الرجعة على سبعين عام” كقول ارما البى واخذوا فى يان يت المقدس كقول جبراييل الملاك لدانيال النبى من غير وقت زمان حد ليم فيه الفراغ منه لانه من افعال الناس بمشيتهم التى8 يتقدمون فيها اوتاخرون او لاعراض تحدث علييم9 تمنعهم وتحبسيم وليس افعال الناس كافعال الله التى تحد فيها الحدود ونوقت10 فيها الازمان كما حدّ لبنى اسراييل المقام بابل سعين عاما. ووقت
the equality was perfect, in accordance with the word of the prophet. The first part, having arrived in Babel in the second year of the reign of Nebuchadnezzar, remained there for the rest of his reign until the end of his life, a duration of 43 years, then five years under the sovereigns of his house, and, after them, 22 years under the domination of Cyrus the Persian - which makes 70 years, in accordance with the word of the prophet. * The other part of the prisoners, having arrived in Babel in the 22nd year * of the reign of Nebuchadnezzar, remained there for the 23 years * of the rest of his reign, then under the reign of his successors 5 years, after that under Cyrus the Persian 31 years, under King Cambyses 8 years, under the Mage one year and under Darius, son of Hystaspes, 2 years, - which makes 70 years. The return was completed at the end of 70 years, as the prophet Jeremiah says. They began to rebuild Jerusalem, in accordance with the word of the angel Gabriel to the prophet Daniel. But they did not finish it at the time they had marked, because the acts of men, in relation to their will, are such that sometimes they precede it, sometimes are late, because of accidental circumstances that prevent and stop them. The acts of men are not similar to those of God, whose terms are fixed * and the time is determined, as He had fixed * for the Israelites the stay in Babel for 70 years, the time of the advent of the Messiah and the term of his death in a determined number of years, because the actions of


[p. 655] {#po.5.p655}
لظهور المسيح ومنتبى قتله عدة سنن معدودة لان افعال الله فى سابق علمه مستورة عن البشش ما لم ينفذ! اظهارها لعبادة فما كان منها فى مناقيعهم اطلع3 عليه انيلا لينادوا ببشارتها وليحرصوا الناس على استدامة حسن اعمليم4 واذا كان بدوهم فى وعيد وتحرير لعقاب ينزله بهم حجنا لسياتهم نادوا بتلك5 واظهروا الاوقات له انذارا لم9 ليردهم الى طاعته ويحرصهم على التوبة وبخونم بالايات الظاهرة فى السمآ والعلامات المبولة وظمم فى رحمة رهم وعطفه عليهم فيضير الله لبم7 فى تلك الاوقات التى توخروا8 فيا فسحة لرجمتهم وتوتهم كما فعل الله فى ايام الطوفان وايام تفرق الالسن وايام سدوم وغامورا وايام نينوى وغيرها!! ولبذه الاوقات استئنى حبراييل الملاك فى قوله لدائيال_النبى ليفهمه فقال" له إلرجعة اولا!! وابنيان ثم من بعد ذلك وقت له سوايع المسيح "من بعد ذلك الوقت فتواات رجعة الجالية من بنى اسرايل الى اليت المقدس بلتين مضت من ملك داريوين ابن بوشتاصف الملك واخذوا فى زمانهم ذلك بالعمل فى نيان المدينة فلم
God in His providence would be hidden from men, if He had not wanted to discover them to His servants; as much as this is useful for them, He informs His prophets so that they may announce the good news and exhort men to perseverance in good actions; if He begins to threaten and to announce the punishment that must strike them in punishment for their sins, the prophets announce it and fix the time of it as a warning, to lead them to obey Him, to push them to repentance, to frighten them by terrible signs that appeared in the sky * and to make them desire the mercy of their Lord and His benevolence toward them. And by adjourning the times, God leaves them the possibility of converting and repenting, as He had done * at the time of the flood, * of the dispersion of languages, of Sodom and Gomorrah, of Nineveh, and other events. And on the subject of this time, the angel Gabriel determined it again in the explanation that he gave of it to the prophet Daniel. He said to him: "First will take place the return, then the construction"; then, after that, he determined for him the weeks of the Messiah from that moment. And the return from exile of the Israelites to Jerusalem occurred 2 years after the accession to the throne of King Darius, son


[p. 656] {#po.5.p656}

of Hystaspes. They began at that time to rebuild the city and did not cease to work there until the time of Artaxerxes Longimanus. * It is written in the Books of Ezra that King Artaxerxes had sent, in the 20th year of his reign, Nehemiah his cupbearer to watch over the construction of Jerusalem; and Nehemiah found that they had already finished it five years before. He found that they were in the process of building * the Temple in the 46th year after the captivity, as the Jewish scholars said to the Messiah: "This temple was built, finished, and completed at the end of 46 years, and You, You say that you will restore it in three days." Yet there is no one who imagines that the Jews were obliged without interruption to the construction of the Temple for 46 years; but, as we have already said, its construction was entirely finished in * the 46th year after the captivity in Babel. Nehemiah left for the king and let him know. One counts the weeks of the Messiah from the time of the end of the construction of the city, according to the word of the angel Gabriel: first the return; then the construction; then one must count the weeks from the 25th year before the end of the reign of King Artaxerxes, and from the end of the construction * until the death of the Messiah; then the total is 483 years, which in sum makes seven weeks and sixty-two weeks of seven (years), as

The prophecy of Jacob, leader of the Patriarchs, and of Moses was realized; they said: "The royal scepter will not be taken from Judah, nor the legislator from between his hands, that is to say the prophets, until he comes who must be his king; * and the peoples will hope in him." We have said that seven weeks and sixty-two weeks make 483 years, because we multiply them by seven. son of Arses, 6 years. The total of the years of the kings of the East is 141 years. After that the count arrives at the years of the Ptolemies, at the years of the kings of the West, and at the enumeration of the happy Ptolemies. * Ptolemy Alexander reigned * 12 years; Ptolemy Lagus, that is to say the word, 40 years; Ptolemy Philadelphus, that is to say who loves his brother, 38 years; it is he who had the sacred Books translated by the Seventy commentators; Ptolemy Euergetes, that is to say the benefactor, 24 years; Ptolemy Philopator, that is to say who loves his father, 17 years; Ptolemy Epiphanes, that is to say the Illustrious, 24 years. Ptolemy Philometor, that is to say who loves his mother, 25 years; again Ptolemy Euergetes, that is to say the benefactor, 19 years; Ptolemy Soter, that is to say the Savior, 12 years; * Ptolemy Alexander Second, 10 years; Ptolemy Philip, that is to say who loves horses, 8 years; Ptolemy Dionysius, that is to say the noble, 30 years; * Cleopatra, that is to say the Glorious, 15 years; Herod, king of the Jews, 35 years. The years of the Messiah Our Lord are 33 years. This gives for the western kings 342 years. The total of the years of the kings of the East and of the West are 483 years. As for the people, they did not know that the Torah had been mutilated. Now that we have already explained and recounted the story of the interpretation and the translation of the Seventy, the efforts and the researches of King Constantine on the reason for the disagreement that he had found in the sacred Books, we are going to return to the account of the time of Abraham; this is the point where we arrived before starting this history. * At the time of Abraham, Khoudroun, brother of Terah, made war on Kisaronos king of Babel, * and having fought and defeated him, he killed him; (he did it), because he wanted to take into captivity the population and plunder the whole country. At that time the empire passed from Babel whose kings were called the Babylonians, to the Assyrians, who reigned over the countries of Mosul, of Nineveh, and the surrounding provinces. Abraham, having lived 100 years, begat Isaac from Sarah; but 16 years before Isaac, he begat Ishmael from Hagar. We begin with Isaac, because the years of the history of the world are counted after Isaac. Abraham begat * from Keturah, after the death of Sarah, a large number of powerful children; * they were called the sons of Cadir (of the Mighty) and their children were also called, because of Ishmael, Modar. Abraham lived, after the birth of Isaac, 75 years and, his life having been 175 years, he died 40 years after the birth of Jacob. At that time died King Belus and after him reigned Ninus, his son, who built the city of Nineveh so called after his name. The fifth year after the birth of Abraham, Jerusalem was built by King Melchizedek. * The first king of the Sicyonians was Aglaous who reigned over them 53 years. In the 71st year after the birth of Abraham broke out the wars and the conflicts between King Chedorlaomer and the five kings of the land of Sodom and Gomorrah; they lasted for 14 years until * the 10th year after the departure of Abraham from the land of Ur of the Chaldeans, which was the land of Canaan, son of Ham, son of Noah. which is al-Ladjoun of the Canaanites. * In the 75th year after the birth of Abraham, * God said to him in the land of Ur of the Chaldeans: "Rise up, go out from the house of your father and from the place of your birth and go into the promised land that God has promised to give to you, to your seed, and to your posterity." It is thus that God concluded the pact and the alliance with Abraham, for him and for his posterity, assuring him the inheritance of the promised land. From this time, one counts 430 years that God assigned to the Israelites for their slavery in the land of Egypt. Other * scholars say * that these years count from the time when Abraham made the sacrifice of a goat, a pigeon, and a turtledove. We have made researches on this subject, but we have found nothing of it. The total of the years from Adam and the beginning of the chronology of the world until this year is 3417 years, because the years from the flood until this year are 1161 years. In the 77th year of his life, Abraham went to Egypt because of the famine and the drought that were raging in the land of Syria. named sons of Hagar. * They were also called Arabs because of the mixed blood of Ishmael with the tribe of Jurhum, because he married women of this tribe and spoke Arabic. They were named Ishmaelites after Ishmael their father. Ishmael having lived 137 years begat from * Arab women twelve princes who are named and mentioned in the Book of the Torah. His death took place 63 years after the birth of Jacob. At that time Lot was taken prisoner by King Chedorlaomer. At the same time Semiramis reigned in Babel for 42 years; * she seized the land of Asia and several cities of Syria, built Babel for the second time, and she raised artificial hills and a large number of monuments that are attributed to her. Abraham having reached the age of 99 years, God ordered him to be circumcised. He was 100 years old completed, when he begat Isaac from Sarah. At the age of 60 years Isaac begat Esau, * who was hairy, and Jacob *, the same as Israel, twins of Rebecca, daughter of Bethuel, relative of Abraham, from a family of Haran. Isaac lived 180 years until the 31st year after the birth of Levi son of Jacob.

* At that time Queen Semiramis built a great temple in a city, on the bank of the Euphrates, to the idol Q.yous (Bel, Baal) and, after having appointed for Q.yous (Bel) seventy priests, she named this city Hierapolis, which means the city of the priests; it was the ancient city of Manbij. When Isaac had reached the age of 16 years, God said to Abraham: "Rise up. Take Isaac, your only son, whom you love, place him on the altar and offer him to God, your Lord, as a burnt offering on the mountain of the Amorites (Moriah)." Later, 1030 years after the birth of Abraham, Solomon, son of David, built a temple of God on * this mountain, the place of the burnt offering of Abraham and of the sacrifice of Isaac. We possess the sacred Books, which recount * and indicate that it is on this mountain, at the place of the burnt offering of Abraham, that Adam was buried and interred; it is said that the body of Adam was with Noah in the ark; when the flood was finished, and Noah with his companions had come out of the ark, he buried Adam on this mountain. Thus God made Abraham walk until He indicated to him the place of the tomb of Adam. Abraham offered Isaac (to God): he built the altar above the tomb of Adam and, Isaac

Having placed himself on the altar raised over the tomb of Adam, he was on the point of immolating him to God as a burnt offering. But God saved him, substituting a lamb for him, because of his trust in God and because He knew the sincerity of Abraham's intention and his trust * that God could restore life to his son after the immolation. It is written that at the moment of the sacrifice of Isaac, when Abraham raised the knife, the hosts of angels clapped their hands and God announced * in heaven the burnt offering of Abraham; (then) He said: "Remove your hand from the child: now I know that you love God, your Lord, with all your heart." The first construction of Jerusalem. After the time of Noah's exit from the ark and before Abraham's settlement in the promised land of the country of Syria, the high priest Melchizedek built on the tomb of our father Adam the city * of Jerusalem. God made known to him and indicated the place of Adam's tomb, and he offered there two sacrifices of bread and wine. Here is what the Jews report in their book which they call the Mishnah: we have heard it said that Hakib and

his followers claim that Melchizedek is the same as Shem, son of Noah, and that Joshua, son of Nun, killed him, along with the thirty-one kings he put to death. But not all Jewish scholars agree with them regarding this folly * and this error. Christians find in the mysteries of their books that the cross upon which the Messiah was crucified was planted in the middle of the tomb of Adam, * in his larynx; it is on this cross that the Messiah was crucified; because of this, this place was called al-tarfgah and it was called Golgotha, which means the skull. In the 19th year of the birth of Isaac, Abraham learned that his brother Nahor, son of Terah, had several children, and (from the children) of Nahor was born Aram, son of Kemuel; he gave his name to the Arameans, who inhabited Haran in Mesopotamia and the surrounding country up to the region of Mosul. We have found books that mention and make known another Aram, descended from Shem, who lived to the east of Susa (Susiana), * opposite Elam, and Asshur, brother of Elam, from whom come the Elamites, the Assyrians * and their tribes. The borders of Aram (extend) from the native land of Shem to the land of Misan; it is for this reason that the population of this country and beyond took the name of Aram their father, descended from the sons of Shem, son of Noah. * In the 37th year of

the birth of Isaac, Sarah died at the age of 127, because she was 90 years old when she gave birth to Isaac for Abraham. In the 44th year of his birth, Isaac married Rebecca, daughter of Bethuel, cousin of his father Abraham. * At that time King Abimelech made friends with Isaac; his kingdom was located in Gerar, in the land of the Jordan. At that time began the domination of the Philistines, who are the inhabitants of Philistia (Palestine); their race indicates the origin of their country. Jacob was 89 years old when he * begat Levi; the life of Jacob was 147 years. In the 20th year of the birth of Jacob, Esau, his brother, married daughters of Canaan, son of Ham, son of Noah; one of them was Judith, daughter of Beeri the Hittite, the other, Basemath, daughter of Elon the Hittite. When Esau had seen * that they did not please his father Isaac, he married Basemath, daughter of Ishmael. It is written that Job the Just drew his origin from the children of Basemath, daughter of Ishmael. It is written that Moses, the prophet, wrote the famous book attributed to Job the Just; in this book, that is to say in the book of Job the Just, there are 1,548 verses.

Job was called in the generation of the tribes and peoples (of Esau) * Jobab, son of Zerah. Job lived 210 years, of which 70 years were before his misfortunes and 140 years after. * We have found among the children of Shem, son of Noah, another Jobab; some people claim that he is the same as Job, because they believed him to be prior to Abraham. This is how one came to affirm contradictory things on this subject. At that time Hamor, brother of Shechem, built a large city which he named Shechem, after the name of his brother. Some time later, two sons of Jacob, Simeon and Levi, devastated it and killed three thousand men there to avenge their sister, who was called Dinah, upon its population. When Jacob had reached the age of 77 and Isaac, his father, that of 137, Isaac, his father, blessed him and addressed in his favor the prayers and blessings which are reported in the Torah; then * he sent him to Haran * to Laban, his uncle, for he feared that Esau would kill him. Jacob left with his staff in his hand. Having arrived at Bethel, he prayed and, having placed his head on a stone, he fell asleep. In the night he saw Angels of God who were ascending and descending along a ladder whose top touched the sky and whose foot was on the earth. During all that night, until the dawn had risen, the Angel wrestled with him. And behold, God manifested Himself to Jacob and said to him: "Jacob,

I will not let you go until you tell me your name." When it was day, Jacob said: "This place is the window of heaven and the house of God", and he made a vow to consecrate to God the tithe of what God had granted him during his absence, to build for Him in this place a house of God. * After that Jacob crossed * the Euphrates with his staff and arrived at Haran. In the 84th year of his life, Jacob married Leah, daughter of Laban, his uncle, * and begat from her Reuben, Simeon, and at the age of 89 he also begat Levi, after whose name the chronology of the world was regulated. Then after Levi he begat Judah, after him Issachar, after him Zebulun. Jacob married Rachel, sister of Leah, and begat from her Joseph and Benjamin. He also begat (two sons) from Bilhah, Rachel's servant, who gave Jacob Gad and Asher, and (two sons) from Zilpah, Leah's servant, who gave Jacob * Dan and Naphtali. They gave birth to the twelve tribes of the Israelites. In the 97th year after the birth of Jacob, he went from Haran to (Isaac), his father; his herds, donkeys, oxen and cows, slaves and utensils were

innumerable. * After his settlement in the promised land of the country of Syria, Joseph was sold by his brothers. At the age of seventeen, he was brought to Egypt where he spent ten years as a slave and three years in prison. Joseph having reached the age of thirty, the Pharaoh, king of Egypt, saw in a dream seven cows and seven ears of grain. * They brought Joseph out of prison and cut his hair; then he was presented before the Pharaoh. Joseph, being thirty years old, explained his dream to him. Everything happened as Joseph had explained it to him. The Pharaoh gave him power over the land of Egypt. In the 39th year of the birth of Joseph, Jacob, his father, arrived in Egypt with all his children and the children of his children in the second year of the famine and drought that were raging in Syria. * The Israelites remained in fact * in Egypt, as slaves, for 215 years. * The number of Israelites who arrived with Jacob in Egypt was 70 men; in addition, Joseph, his two sons, Ephraim and Manasseh, and their two sons, (which makes) 5 men. Jacob, being 127 years old, presented himself before the Pharaoh 7 years after the death of Isaac, his father. The number of Israelites, when they had left Egypt, was 603,500 men capable of bearing arms who were counted in the census, that is to say in the census (of Moses). When Moses

had counted and separated them, he did not register and did not count those who were under 22 years old and those * who were over 50 years old; these were not counted, nor were the women. Jacob having lived in Egypt for 20 years, died 13 * years after the birth of Kohath. Levi, having lived 45 years, begat Kohath, and Kohath, having lived 60 years, begat Amram. At that time the Pharaoh, sovereign of Joseph, having died, it was Amosis (Ἅμωσις) who reigned after him in Egypt for 25 years. In the 38th year of the birth of Kohath, reigned in Egypt Hebron (Xe&pwv) for 13 years. At that time * Zeus, whose name translates to Jupiter (al-Mushtari), knew Niobe and begat from her Apis, who was named later Serapis and who began to fight the courageous women (7), who were called the Amazons and killed the males of their children. Zeus reigned over the country of Crete by the sea. The translation of Zeus * is "a long life", because it is said that he lived a thousand years. At that time the city of Eleusina was built. In the 51st year of the birth of Kohath, son of Levi, ascended the

فرعون فملك احدى وعشرين سئة ويذا جبنيذ بِضْرًا يلى اسرابيل وعاش عمران سبعين ست فولد له موسى انبى وعاش من بعد ان ولد له موسى سبعة وستين سنة وات عمران قبل خروج بنى اسرايل من مصر ملثة عش سنة؟ وفى سنة ستة من مولد عمران مات يوسف وهو ابن ماية سنة وعشر سنين واوضى بثى اسراييل ان يحملوا معهم عظامه3 اذا ذكرهم الله واخرجم من ارض مصر4 مكتوب انه صار فى ذلك الزمان طوفان فى ايام اغوس وفى سنة اننى عشر ملك بمصر منزس5 اننا عش سنة وفى سنة اربعة وعشرين من مولد عمران ملك ايض يليوس ثمان عشر سنة وفى سئة ثمان وستين من امول عمران ملك بمصر امنافيس7 ثاثة وارسين سنة وامسر ان يخنق كل ذكر من اللاد أنى سراي وخرقونا فى النيل
throne of Egypt Amenophis (Ἀμεμφῆς) and was also called Pharaoh; * he reigned 21 years and then began to persecute the Israelites. Amram, having lived 70 years, begat Moses the prophet and, having lived after the birth of Moses 67 years, he died 13 years before the exodus of the Israelites from Egypt. In the 6th year of the birth of Amram died Joseph, at the age of 110. His brothers ordered the Israelites to take with them his bones, when God would recall them and bring them out of the land of Egypt *. It is written that at that time, under (King) Ogyges (Ὥγυγος), the flood took place. In the 12th year (after the birth of Amram) reigned in Egypt * Mensis (Apevoñç) 12 years. In the 24th year after the birth of Amram reigned also Balaios for 18 years. In the 68th year after the birth of Amram, reigned in Egypt Amenophis (Asfanis, Μισφρῆς7) 43 years; he ordered the strangling of the firstborn males of the Israelites and their drowning in the river Nile.

مولد موسى الى وخرء! فلما ولد موسى لعمران ايه وكان عمران يوم ولد مودى ابن سبعين سنة طرحه ابواه فى نهر النيل وصيراه فى صندوقة وهو ابن ثاثة اشهر وكان له حمال وحسن وفى ذلك الزمان خرحت مرى9 ينثت فرعون الى نهر النيل فاصابت موسى فلما راته مختونا7 علمت انه من بنى اسراييل فاخذته مرى ورته ونتبته8 وعلمته جميع؟ علوم المصريين واداهم وحكمتهم"! وكان معلماا يائيس وامبريس !! فصار موسى عظيم الغان بعص كبير القدر وفى سنة ثمان وعشرين من مولد موسى بنا فرعون هربوبولس المدينة على نهر انيل التى هى الفرماة! وف ذلك الزيان حارب الحشة اهل مصي واخربوا كثير من بلاد مص ويا حينيذ ُنفرا الملك: يد موسى هو وجميع اصحابه
The birth of Moses and his history. When Moses came into the world, Amram, his father, was 70 years old on the very day of his birth. His parents, having put him in a basket, exposed him on the river Nile. * He was only three months old and he was pretty and beautiful. At that time Mary, daughter of the Pharaoh, went out toward the river Nile where she found Moses. When she had seen him circumcised, she knew that he was of the Israelites. Mary, having taken him, raised him, instructed him * and taught him all the sciences of the Egyptians, their education * and their wisdom. Jannes and Jambres were the teachers of Moses, who became, in Egypt, strong and powerful. In the 28th year of the birth of Moses the prophet, the Pharaoh built the city of Hermopolis on the river Nile * which was also called al-F.r.ma. At that time the Ethiopians fought the Egyptians and devastated several provinces of Egypt. Then Kenefra, the king, all his companions, his notables and his relatives envied Moses, but because of Mary, neither he nor they could arrive at what they wanted regarding him; then they tried to make him lose his dignity


[p. 674] {#po.5.p674}
وخاصتة وقرايية فلمكان مرى لم يمكنة واباهم منه ما اراذوا فاغتالوه من طريق "مريت وموضع راسته! فقالوا له ان للملكة عليك حقا عظيما وان الحبشة يركبون النيل فى السفن عند صعوده وشزون ارض مص وقد خريوا بلدانا كثيرة وسبوا اهلبا وقد يجب عليك نصرة المملكة4 لتقديمها اياك وحتها عليك وقد راى الملك وجميع قواده وخاسته ان تغزوا الحبشة وتقال ان جميع البرادى واففاد؟ النى فيدا بين ارض الحيشة وأيرض مص من بلاد التبه ارض ليست بمسكونة وانها ارض كثيرة الثعاين والحيات لايقام لسبيلبا7 فلما علم موسى اغتاليم اياد وما عضو له8 حينيذ امن موسى فانتخب من الى اسر ايل عشر الف فارس ومن المصريين" عشر الف فارس م امس فاتخذد له من الكراكى العظام وهى التى تسما القعاقع عدة كثيرة لاتحصى فصتّرها!! فى اقفاس مشبكة تخرج رونا من تلك الاقفاص ففزا موسى باصحابه وكان يامر ان يطعم تلك المراكى فى اولك انار فاذا كان الليل ونزل بساكرة امن “ فتفرقت تلك الاقفاص التى
and his powerful situation, saying to him: "You have great obligations toward the queen. Behold, the Ethiopians are descending the Nile in boats thanks to the flood and are attacking the land of Egypt; they have already ravaged several provinces and have reduced their population to captivity. It is your duty to defend the kingdom, because you are placed at its head and its defense rests on you. * The king, all his chiefs and his court are of the opinion that you attack the Ethiopians. It is said that all the lands and deserts that are between Ethiopia and Egypt are not inhabited, the country being full of a great quantity of snakes, which prevent passing through it." When Moses had learned of their traps and what they were plotting against him, he ordered to choose from among the Israelites 10,000 horsemen and the same number from among the Egyptians; then he ordered to prepare for him the greatest possible number of large cranes (ibises), which were called ka 'ka', and to put them in woven cages, * so that their heads showed out of the cages. Moses set out on the march with his companions. He had these cranes fed at daybreak and, when night fell and he had set up camp, he ordered * that the cages where the cranes were be distributed throughout his camp. During


[p. 675] {#po.5.p675}
فيا الكراكى فى جميع عسكرة فكات تصيح الليل اجمع من شدة جوعها فتسمع صوتبها الحيات والثعابين تتغّب! فى الارض من خونا لان الكراكى غداها الحيات وطعامها فلم يزل كذلك حتى وصل3 الى موره مدينة الحشة فلما راود اهلها وماينوا حيوشه اعظمم ذلك وهاليم كبر عندهم وعجبوا من وصول المساكن والجيوش اليهم فى تلك القفارة فقدف الله8 فى قاب نت ملك الحثة وقزر فى عقلبا أن موسى سيظفى بالمدينة7 ومن فيا وان الله تارك سيسلطه عليهم فبثت الى موسى تعرض نفسها عليه ان يتزوحها لتدله8 على موضع فتح المديئة ومخادع نواحيها فانعم لها بذلك واظف» الله بالمدينة ومن فيها من اهلبا وتزوج موسى نت ملك الحبشة ولذلك يقال فى الكتاب ان مريم اخت موسى وهرون" تكلمت فى موسى وطعنت عليه من اجل انه تزووج أمراة كوشانية !! اعنى من ولد الحشة وان الله غضب على مريم فاجريت وامتلت برصا فاخرحها شعب بني اسراييل من ين الجموع فرحمبا موسى تى الله وتضرع
the night, tormented by hunger, all the cranes let out resounding cries. * The snakes, having heard their cries, fled into the earth for fear that the cranes would eat them. Moses did not cease doing this until he reached the Ethiopian city of Meroe. When the inhabitants had seen him with his troops, this struck them with admiration and fear, * and they were astonished that so many troops had arrived at their place through these deserts. Then God inspired the daughter of the king of Ethiopia and made her know with certainty that Moses would seize the city and its inhabitants and that God, may He be blessed, would make him master of it. This is why she sent word to Moses that she offered herself to him so that he might marry her; (in return) she would show him a place from which one could take the city and seize its surroundings. * Moses granted it to her. God having given him victory over the city and its inhabitants, Moses married the daughter of the king of Ethiopia. This is why the Holy Scripture relates that Miriam, sister of Moses, and Aaron, in speaking of Moses, slandered him, because * he had married a Cushite woman, that is to say of the sons of al-Khabaschat (an Ethiopian). God was irritated against Miriam, who became scabby and leprous, so that the Israelites removed her from them.


[p. 676] {#po.5.p676}
الى الله من احلبا فقال الله لموسى لو ان ابإها! برق ليا فى وحها لكان يشغى لبا ان تستحى عشرة ايام وقال ان موسى اقام من بعد فتح المدينة فى بلاد الحشة بشاكرة وجيوشه وجنده الى وقت زمان صعود النيل فنتحمل حبنيذ بجنودة ومساكره فى السفن الى مص مع صعود النيل وكثرة الما واتى الملك3 بالفتح العظيم فند ذلك هابه الملك وجميع خاصته واصحابه ووزرايه وجميع اهل مصرة وهم الملك بقتله حيث مانت مرى التى كات ارت موسى9 وفى سنةاسعة وثلثين من مولد موسى ولد بشوع7 ان نون وفى سنة احد8 وارسين من مولد موسى ملك بمص فرعون الذى يذكر الكتاب انه غرق مع جنودلا وحبوشه ومراكيه فى البحر بعد ارسين سنة كالذى هو مكتوب انه لما صار موسى ابن9! ارسين سنة قتل!1 ذلك الرجل المصرى الذى اراد قتله19 وهرب الى بلاد مدين الى رعواييل الذى هو إثرون3! وهو سعيب! فتزوج موسى صفورا5ا بنت سعيب فولد له منها جرسون ل والعازر وفى سنة اننين وارعين من
But Moses, the prophet of God, having pity on her, prayed to God for her. And God said to Moses: "If her father had spat in her face, would she not be covered with shame for ten days?" Moses stayed, after the conquest of the city, in the lands of Ethiopia, with all his troops, until the time of the flooding of the Nile; * then he brought his troops back to Egypt, by means of boats during the flood * of the Nile and the high waters, and he arrived at the king's place laden with rich booty. In the meantime the king, all his notables, his courtiers, his ministers and all the inhabitants of Egypt were afraid of him, and the king even conceived the idea of killing Moses, after the death * of Mary who had raised Moses. In the 37th year after the birth of Moses, was born Joshua, son of Nun. In the 41st year of the birth of Moses, ascended the throne of Egypt the Pharaoh who, according to the Sacred Books, was submerged in the sea, 40 years later, with his troops and his chariots. * According to what is written, Moses, being 40 years old, killed an Egyptian who wanted to put him to death, and he fled to the land of Midian to Raguel or Jethro or Sa'ib and, having married Zipporah, daughter of Sa'ib, he begat Gershom and Eliezer.


[p. 677] {#po.5.p677}
مولد موسى ولد كالب ابن يوفنا! وفى ذلك الزمان ظبر وعرف اطاس فيمشوس معلم السحر وقال انه بلغ سجر الى فلك السما وعرف جميع ما فيها من معانى اسرار الهلاك وسمونه ايضا من هذد الملة. الجبل الذى. فوق السحاب ومن علمه وكتبه صر الى الحكيم اشكال الفلك وجميع انواعه بصورها وحدودها وهو الكتاب الذى تقل الطاهر ابن الحسين ذى البمينين بصور وحجميغ اشكاله وانواع معانيه من الرومية الى العربية وكتاب عجيب وأن طلب وجد وفى ذلك الزمان عرف سورس الملك وكان ملكه على دمشق وانما سميت الشام سورية على اسمه استوا لها اسم من اسم سوس وفى سنة ثمانين من مولد موسى كان4 بشوع " ابن نونة ابن تسع وثاثين سنة تجلى_الله على موسى بطورسينا واراه الله المجايب النى» فمل فى المصاة وفى يده" التي اببيضت وغير ذلك مما عمله الله ثم ان الله أمرلا ونه ؟ ان يعمل العجابب والايات بارض مصر ومخرج "1 بنى اسراييل منها " وفى تلك السنة اخرج موسى بنى اسراييل من ارض مصر!!
* In the 42nd year of the birth of Moses, was born Caleb, son of Jephunneh. At that time appeared and made himself known Atlas, son of Prometheus, who taught magic (astrology). It is said that, his magic reaching the celestial sphere, he knew everything that happened there of its mysteries. Because of this, the name of Atlas was given to the mountain that rises above the clouds. * According to his knowledge and his books, Eratosthenes, the scholar, traced the plans of the sphere with all the species of its constellations and their terms. This book was translated from Greek into Arabic by Thahir ibn al-Husayn, a man ingenious with his maps, all his plans and all the species of his subtleties. It is a remarkable book and, if one wishes, one can find it. At that time was known King Souris, who reigned over Damascus (Dimeschk). As for why Syria (ash-Sham) is called Souriah, this name is derived from the name of Souris. * In the 80th year of the birth of Moses, Joshua, son of Nun, being 39 years old, God manifested Himself to Moses at Tur-Sina (at Sinai) and made him see the miracles that He performed with his staff and with his hand, which had become white, and other things that God accomplished. Then God ordered him to go to Egypt to perform miracles and celestial signs and to bring out


[p. 678] {#po.5.p678}
حيث ضرب البح بعصلا واحاز بنى اسراييل البح فلما جاوزوا! ضرب ايضًا بصلا البحر فزق الله فرعون وجميع حنوده وتمت الكلمة التى قال الله لابراهيم ان زرعك تكون فى ارض العودية فى بلاد الغرية ارمع ماية ا «ائين سن
the Israelites. In this year Moses brought the Israelites out of Egypt; he struck the sea with his staff and made the Israelites pass through the sea; when they had passed, he struck the sea again with his staff, and God submerged the Pharaoh and all his troops. Then was realized the word * that God had said to Abraham, that his posterity would remain in a distant land in a state of slavery for 430 years.

* Explanation of the causes and reasons that led to the introduction of the worship of idols in Egypt.
يقال ان المصريين الذين لم يخرجوا مع فرعون ممن كان بقى بمصر من اهلها حيث9 بلفهم خبر فرعون انه قد7 غرق هو وجنودد فى البحر؟ عمد كل واحد منهم عند ذلك فد الشى الذى كان فى يده من عمله وصناعته وصيّر كل واحد منهم بشبه ذلك الشى الذى كان فى .يذه صنما ووناا وسجد له كانه هو الذى خلصه من الغرق ومن هذا العلة كثرت عادة الاوثان وسجدة الاصنام بمصر"! وحيث طال بم الزمان والدهر كلمتهم الشيالطين من تلك الاصنام !1
It is said that all the Egyptians who had not gone out with the Pharaoh and who had remained in Egypt, having learned the news that the Pharaoh and his troops had drowned in the Red Sea, * were struck by it; * and they worshipped the thing and the object that they held in their hand, and each of them made an idol in the likeness of this object and worshipped it, as if it were that which had saved them from submersion. Because of this the worship of idols in Egypt increased and, after a certain time, devils spoke to them from


[p. 679] {#po.5.p679}
وق تلك السنة التى هى اخد كَثمائن من “ مولد مولي حبك خرج إلى اسرائل من ارض مص حارهم العمالقة فاباد الله من العمالقة على يدى موسى خلقا كثيرا ودامت العداوة بين العمالقة وين بى اسرايل الى زمان هامان العمليقى وزذر ارمطحشكت الملك! ومنانى بقصته فى موضعها ان شنا الله ا وفى تلك السئة صعد موسى الى أجل الله بطورسينا فى "الغهر انالك فقل من الله المعرفة واعطاد الله الحكمة والنبوة والعلم ان يبدا فيتكلم على ابتدآ الخلق وكيفيّة العالم وان بخبر ويحدث ان العالم مخلوق مصنوع وان للعالم ابتدا وذلك انه يقال انه لم يكن الدهريه فى زمان من الازمنة ودهر من الادهار منذ بدى العالم أكثر منهم فى ذلك العص النى وضع فيه موسئ نى الله التورية على ابتدا الخلق حتى اعتقد عامة الناس القول بالدهر فان كثير من بنى اسراييل اعتقدوا ذلك الراى وقالوا بذلك القول وهو كان داليم وايمانهم وان موسى تكلم على مسكن الزمان ايضا" ومن بعد تلك السنة اقام موسى على مسكن؟9 الزمان كبيرا7 بنى اسراييل8 من الاتى عشر سبطا قراينهم فى مسكن الزمان
the inside of these idols. In the 81st year after * the birth of Moses, when the Israelites had gone out of Egypt, the Amalekites attacked them; but God caused a great number of them to perish through the intermediary of Moses. * The hostility between the Amalekites and the Israelites continued until the time of Haman the Amalekite, minister of King Artaxerxes. We will report the story of this in its place, if God wills it. In this year, in the third month, Moses went up to the mountain of God, Tur-Sina (Sinai), and received from God the knowledge of things. God granted him wisdom, prophecy and science, so that he could speak of the origin of the world and explain the universe, so that he would make known and explain that the universe was created and had a beginning; * it is said that in the space of the centuries that have passed, since the origins of the world, there had not been as many partisans of the temporal origin as at the time when Moses, the prophet of God, composed * the Torah, in which he dealt with the origin of the world, to bring men to the opinion that the world was temporal. And most of the Israelites adhered firmly to this belief and professed this doctrine; this was their belief and their religious conviction. Moses also spoke of the Tabernacle. From this year on, Moses appointed to the Tabernacle the oldest of the Israelites, chosen from twelve tribes, who governed the Tabernacle.


[p. 680] {#po.5.p680}
فجميع السنين من ادم وابتدا الخلق الى السنة التى صعد فيا مونى الى جيل الله بطورسينا وقبل الواح! التورة وهى سنة احد: وثمانين من مولد موسى يكون ملغ السنين من ادم الى تلك السنة ثلثة الاف وثمان ماية وسبع وارعين سنة وهذا كان غرضنا بديا فى اول قولنا4 وكُتابا وقد صحخناها واستقصينا حسايها وشرئلا يما تقتم من نعتنا واول فصح صنعوا الود فى مصر كان يوم الاحد في كيكلوس التاسع وفى مت الارعين سنة التى اقاموا فى البرية ما عملوا فيه فصحة ثم دبرهم موسى بعد تلك السنة. سعة ؤائين سنة وتوفى وهو ابن ماية وعشرين سنة فلغت السنين من ادم وستدا الخلق الى ألسثة التى توفى فيها موسى. لشة الف سنة وثمان .ماية وستة ومائين سنة7 وصارت السنين من .بعد ذلك تحسب ما بتّثلا من تاريخ العالم على سنين تدبير القضاة اياهم8 ويان ذلك ان مبلغ السنين من ادم الى الطوفان الفان ومايتان وستة وخسين. سنة ومن الطوفان الى أن ولد لارغوا؟ الفالغ الذى19 تقسمت!! الالسن فى
The total of the years from Adam and the beginning of the world until the year when Moses went up to * the mountain of God Tur-Sina (Sinai) and received the tables of the Torah, that is to say until the year 81 after the birth of Moses, - the total of the years from Adam until that year is * 3847 years. We have exposed all this at the beginning of our treatise and our book; we have corrected it * by abbreviating the calculation and have explained it higher up. The Jews celebrated the first Passover in Egypt on the Sunday of the ninth cycle; and they did not celebrate the Passover during the 40 years they spent in the desert. Then Moses governed them, after this year, 39 years and died at the age of 120. The years from Adam and the origins of the world until the year of the death * of Moses are 3886 years. After that the years are counted, as we have explained, in the history of the world, according to the years of the government of the Judges over the Israelites. The explanation is as follows: the total of the years from Adam until the flood is 2256 years; from the flood until that year when he had begotten Peleg, at the time of whom the languages were divided, * - 670 years; from


[p. 681] {#po.5.p681}
ايامه ستماية وسعين سنة! ومن ادم الى تلك السنة الفان سنة وتسع ماية وستة وعشرين سنة ومن .يوم ولد ادغوا الى يوم ولد ابراهيم ارعماية وسثة عشر سنة ومن الطوفان الى تلك السنة الف وستة وسبعين سنة ومن ادم الى تلك السنة ثلثة الاف ونلثة ماية وائنين وارسين سنة3 ومن يوم ولد موسى الى ان خرج نو اسراايل من مص وهى السنة التى صعد فها الى حبل الله بطورسينا وقبل الالواح من الله احد4 وثمائين سنة من الطوفان الى تلك السنة الف وخمس ماية واحد وتسعين سنة ومن ادم الى تلك السنة لئة الف وثمان ماية وسبعة وارسين سنة على ما تقدم فى قولنا" ثم دبرهم موسى بعد ذلك ايام حيانه وشتّة عمره تسعة وئاثين سنة فاتبت السنين من ادم وببتدا الخلق الى السنة التى توفى فها موسى ثلثة الافى ونمان ماية وسئة وثمانين سنة وال فضح عبلوا الهود بعد ارسين سنة .فى زمان يشوع ابن نون على اريحا؟ فى كيكلوس العاشرة ومن بعد ذلك دبرهم بشوع أبن نون سبعة وعشرين سنة ثم من بعد بشوع ابن نون دبر نى اسراييل المدبرون والقضاة تمام خمس مابة اسلة وست وخسين بسنة ومن بعد انقضا مدبرييم تحسب تاريخ السنين لتاريخ العالم على سنى ملوكهم ومتدى تاريخها من
Adam until that year 2926 years; from the day of the birth of Ar'a until the day of the birth of Abraham 416 years; from the flood until that year 1076 * (1510) years; from Adam until that year 3342 (3766) years; from the day of the birth of Moses until the exodus of the Israelites from Egypt, that is to say until the year when Moses went up to the mountain of God Tur-Sina and received the tables of God, - 81 years; from the flood until that year 1591 years; from Adam until that year 3847 years, according to what we have said higher up. After that Moses governed, during his life until he died, * the space of 39 years, so that the years from Adam and the origins of the world until the year of the death of Moses are 3886 years. The Jews celebrated the first Passover 40 years later, at the time of Joshua, son of Nun, near Jericho, in the tenth cycle. After Moses, it was Joshua, son of Nun, who governed them for 27 years; then after Joshua, son of Nun, it was the governors and the judges who governed the Israelites for 556 years; after the end of their government the chronology of the history of the world is evaluated according to the years of their kings. This chronology begins with the year when ascended the throne Saul,


[p. 682] {#po.5.p682}
السنة التى ملك فيها شاووك! ابن ا من سط بيامين وهو اود ملك ملك على بنى اسرايل فتكون السنين من" ادم وستدا الخلق الى اليوم الذى ملك فيه شاوول ازعة الف وبلثماية واحد+ وتسعين سنة “, وكانت احياة موسى ماية وعشرين سنة منها ارين سين مقانه بمصر وينها ادحين سنة مشامه فى مدين عند ثرون وبنها ارعين سنة مامه فى التبه وكتب موسى خسة ابفار كالذى نقتم من قينا وفى تلك الاسفار بع عر الف
son of Kish, of the tribe of Benjamin, the first king who reigned over the Israelites. The years from Adam * and the origins of the world until the day when Saul ascended the throne are 4391 years. The life of Moses was 120 years, of which he spent 40 years in Egypt, 40 years with Jethro in the land of Midian and 40 years in the desert. Moses wrote five books, of which we have spoken higher up; in these books there are 17,041 verses.
